TrailingMode 4 semantics confirmed:
```csharp
else if (ProjectileID.Sets.TrailingMode[type] == 4)
{
    Vector2 vector3 = Main.player[owner].position - Main.player[owner].oldPosition;   // player movement this frame
    for (int num31 = oldPos.Length - 1; num31 > 0; num31--)
    {
        oldPos[num31] = oldPos[num31 - 1];
        oldRot[num31] = oldRot[num31 - 1];
        oldSpriteDirection[num31] = oldSpriteDirection[num31 - 1];
        if (numUpdates == 0 && oldPos[num31] != Vector2.Zero)
        {
            oldPos[num31] += vector3;   // player-translation compensation
        }
    }
    oldPos[0] = position;
    oldRot[0] = rotation;
    oldSpriteDirection[0] = spriteDirection;
}
```

Note: this runs per-update (including extraUpdates), so with extraUpdates=1 the trail cache shifts twice per frame; numUpdates==0 check applies compensation only on the first update of the frame (player position delta computed once per frame).

```
Now I have our current implementation. Issues to fix vs vanilla:

**Our current trail** (draw at 704-728):
1. `ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'` — vanilla uses **AlphaBlend** (standard alpha). WRONG.
2. Polyline with `lineWidth = prof.len / 2` — vanilla total strip width = 2 × trailWidth = 2 × (len/2) = len. So lineWidth should be `prof.len` (full blade length), not len/2. WRONG (half width).
3. Color fade: our `fade = (i+1)/n` where i=0 is OLDEST (trail[0] is oldest since we push and splice from front... wait: `this.trail.push(cx, cy, rot)` appends to END; `splice(0, len - 180)` removes from FRONT. So trail[0] = oldest, trail[end] = newest. Our loop: `fade = (i+1)/n` — i=0 (oldest) fade≈0, newest fade≈1. Vanilla: progress = i/(N-1) where i=0 is NEWEST with color factor 1, i→N-1 oldest with factor (1 - GetLerpValue(0, 0.98, progress)). So our fade direction matches (oldest→0, newest→1) BUT the exact formula differs: vanilla factor = 1 - lerp01(0, 0.98, progress), where progress = i/(N-1). Our (i+1)/n ≈ linear 0→1 — close but not exact: vanilla is 1 - (progress/0.98) clamped, which reaches 0 at progress 0.98, not 1.0. Also vanilla alpha = 255*(factor)/2 → alpha fraction = factor/2 = 0.5*factor. Our a = 0.5*fade*opacity — matches approximately. But we should use the exact formula.
4. Vanilla RGB also multiplied by the fade factor (Color * float multiplies RGB too!). Our strokeStyle uses full RGB with alpha fade only. Vanilla: rgb * factor AND alpha = 255*factor/2. So the tail color darkens AND fades. Need to multiply RGB by factor.
5. Missing the current position as segment: vanilla strip starts at i=0 which is the CURRENT position (oldPos[0] = position set this update). Our trail stores positions pushed during step() — the last entry IS current. OK.
6. **Missing the afterimages!** Vanilla draws 4 white afterimages of the sword sprite at oldPos[15/30/45/60] with `color34 = White * lerpValue3 * Opacity * num170` (lerpValue3 = (60-num159)/60, num170 = envelope at historical counter). Our implementation has NO afterimages. That's a big part of the Zenith look (multiple sword ghosts trailing behind).
7. **Missing DrawPrettyStarSparkle at the sword tip** — the 4-pointed star twinkle (Extra[98]) at tip, white core + colored shine, appearing between counter 15 and 45, with pulse. We don't have it.
8. Trail drawn as centered polyline along path centers — vanilla ribbon cross-section is ALONG THE BLADE AXIS (perpendicular-ish to motion, rotating with the sword), not perpendicular to motion. A centered polyline of width len approximates a swath but the cross-section orientation differs. Vanilla: each vertex pair = oldPos[i].center ± bladeDir(rot[i]) * trailWidth. The ribbon connects these pairs. For a canvas approximation, I should draw per-segment quads: for each consecutive pair (i, i+1), quad = (p_i + d_i*w, p_{i+1} + d_{i+1}*w, p_{i+1} - d_{i+1}*w, p_i - d_i*w) where d = bladeDir at that sample, w = trailWidth = len/2. Fill with the segment color (average or per-vertex gradient — canvas linear gradient between the two cross-section colors!).

Actually — I can do proper vertex-color gradient: for each quad, create a linearGradient from p_i to p_{i+1} with color_i and color_{i+1}. That reproduces per-vertex color interpolation of the strip.

9. Lighting: our lightRGB = trailColor * 0.5 * Opacity at sword center — that matches `Lighting.AddLight(base.Center, vector5 * 0.5f * Opacity)`. But vanilla ALSO adds `Lighting.AddLight(mountedCenter, Lerp(white, trailColor, 0.7) * Opacity * 0.15)`. And only while num2 < 1 (counter/60 < 1). Our lightRGB is set every step regardless of phase. Need to gate on phase < 1 and add the mounted-center light... but lightRGB is a single light attached to the entity. Let me check how lightRGB is consumed — is there a mechanism for the player-centered light? Let me check how lightRGB is used in the engine and whether the owner player has a light contribution mechanism. The task says "Game 实体点光扫描" — let me grep lightRGB usage.

10. Dust: our dust spawns via `game.spawnParticles(...)` with `1/9 × num11` probability — WRONG: vanilla calls dustMethod num11 TIMES per update, each having 1/9 chance. So probability should be `1 - (8/9)^num11` approximated by calling num11 times each 1/9. Our single roll `Math.random() < num11/9` is an approximation. Also when spawning, vanilla spawns 1-3 dust (Next(1,4)) not 1. Also dust color = Lerp(trailColor, White, rand*0.3), scale 0.4, fadeIn, noGravity, velocity along blade-perpendicular direction (3+rand*4). Our version: 1 particle, color = raw trailColor (no white lerp), life 20, no velocity along the direction. Let me check spawnParticles signature to see if we can pass velocity. Also vanilla dust type 278 — check what dust 278 looks like (it's a "flowy" dust?). Let me check our spawnParticles API.

Also the sparkle uses Extra[98] texture. Do we have that asset? terraria-assets — Extra_98. Let me check what's available. Also for the trail, the shader uses Extra_201/Extra_193 noise textures — we can't do the shader, but maybe we can add subtle texture. Let me check if Extra_98/201/193 exist in our assets.

Also — draw order: vanilla draws (1) trail first? No wait — DrawProj: the FinalFractalHelper.Draw (trail) is called at 28040 — but the main sprite draw happens later in the same DrawProjDirect (line 30803+)? Wait, the main draw at 30803 is in a different method? 28040 is within DrawProjDirect (starting ~27970?) and 30803 also within DrawProjDirect? That method is huge (27970→30850+). 251	/** Extra_N 贴图懒加载（sprites/vanilla/Extra_N.png；Image→Bitmap 升级同 projSprite） */
252	export function loadExtraSprite(n: number): ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
253	  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
254	  const hit = extraSpriteCache.get(n);

273	  const hit = sparkleTintCache.get(key);
274	  if (hit) return hit;
275	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
276	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
277	  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
278	  cx.drawImage(img as CanvasImageSource, 0, 0);
279	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
280	  cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${rgb[0]},${rgb[1]},${rgb[2]})`;
281	  cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
282	  // alpha 摊平（预乘语义：原贴图 alpha 不参与 src 贡献强度）
283	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
284	  cx.fillStyle = '#fff';
285	  cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
286	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
287	  if (sparkleTintCache.size > 64) sparkleTintCache.clear();
288	  sparkleTintCache.set(key, c);
289	  return c;
290	}
291	
292	/** DrawPrettyStarSparkle 1:1（Main.cs:34734-34750）。
293	 *  envelope num = GetLerpValue(fadeInStart,fadeInEnd,flare) × GetLerpValue(fadeOutEnd,fadeOutStart,flare)
294	 *  （Utils.cs:283-308 含 from>to 反向钳位）；vector=(fatnessX·0.5, scaleX)·num、
295	 *  vector2=(fatnessY·0.5, scaleY)·num；四笔全 lighter 加色：
296	 *  ①shine 旋 π/2+rotation @vector ②shine 旋 rotation @vector2
297	 *  ③draw 旋 π/2+rotation @vector·0.6 ④draw 旋 rotation @vector2·0.6。
298	 *  color = shine×opacity×0.5（A=0）、color2 = draw×0.5——A=0 预乘语义 = 纯加色，
299	 *  亮度全部折进 tint·globalAlpha（原版 (int) 截断舍入差 ≤1/255 不逐笔复刻）。
300	 *  消费端：402 星尘蠕虫头（:23888）/SwingArc 真刃弧族（:27424 族）/425 枪口星芒。 */
301	export function prettyStarSparkle(
302	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
303	  opacity: number, x: number, y: number,
304	  drawColor: readonly [number, number, number], shineColor: readonly [number, number, number],
305	  flareCounter: number,
306	  fadeInStart: number, fadeInEnd: number, fadeOutStart: number, fadeOutEnd: number,
307	  rotation: number, scale: readonly [number, number], fatness: readonly [number, number],
308	  drawDim = 1, shineDim = 1,
309	): void {
310	  const lv = (from: number, to: number, t: number): number => {
311	    if (from < to) { if (t < from) return 0; if (t > to) return 1; }
312	    else { if (t < to) return 1; if (t > from) return 0; }
313	    return (t - from) / (to - from);
314	  };
315	  const num = lv(fadeInStart, fadeInEnd, flareCounter) * lv(fadeOutEnd, fadeOutStart, flareCounter);
316	  if (num <= 0) return;
317	  const shine = sparkleTintedTex(shineColor);
318	  const draw = sparkleTintedTex(drawColor);
319	  if (!shine || !draw) return;
320	  const tw = shine.width, th = shine.height;
321	  const v1x = fatness[0] * 0.5 * num, v1y = scale[0] * num;
322	  const v2x = fatness[1] * 0.5 * num, v2y = scale[1] * num;
323	  const aShine = 0.5 * opacity * num * shineDim, aDraw = 0.5 * num * drawDim;
324	  // drawDim/shineDim：原版调用侧预乘进色的因子（如 SwingArc 的 White×num3×0.5、
325	  // 973 的 shine×num13）——折进 globalAlpha 而非逐帧染色（防 tintCache 打爆）
326	  const stroke = (img: HTMLCanvasElement, rot: number, sw: number, sh: number, alpha: number) => {
327	    if (alpha <= 0 || sw <= 0 || sh <= 0) return;
328	    ctx.save();
329	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
330	    ctx.translate(x, y);
331	    ctx.rotate(rot);
332	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
333	    ctx.drawImage(img, -tw * sw / 2, -th * sh / 2, tw * sw, th * sh);
334	    ctx.restore();
335	  };
336	  stroke(shine, Math.PI / 2 + rotation, v1x, v1y, aShine);
337	  stroke(shine, rotation, v2x, v2y, aShine);
338	  stroke(draw, Math.PI / 2 + rotation, v1x * 0.6, v1y * 0.6, aDraw);
339	  stroke(draw, rotation, v2x * 0.6, v2y * 0.6, aDraw);
340	}
341

344	import { ITEM_DEFS, ITEM_BY_KEY, VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID } from '../data/items';
345	import { statOfInternal } from '../data/vanillaItemStats';
346	import { townExtraFrames, TOWN_NPC_HEAD_INDEX, BOSS_HEAD_INDEX, bossHeadRotation, VANILLA_NPCS } from '../data/vanillaNpcs';

  /** GameHooks.addLightAt：世界坐标光源（翅膀 28 微光等实体侧 AddLight） */
  addLightAt(x: number, y: number, r: number, g: number, b: number): void {
    const tx = Math.floor(x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(y / TILE);
    if (this.world?.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) this.lighting?.addLight(tx, ty, r, g, b);
  }

  lightAt(x: number, y: number): [number, number, number] | null {
    if (!this.lighting) return null;
    return this.lighting.lightAt(Math.floor(x / TILE), Math.floor(y / TILE));
  }

98:  spawn(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, type: number,
145:    const c = this.spawn(rf.x, rf.y, 5, 5, rf.type, rf.vx, rf.vy, rf.alpha, rf.color, 1);
  spawn(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, type: number,
    speedX = 0, speedY = 0, alpha = 0, color: DustColor | null = null, scale = 1): VDust | null {
    // 屏外裁剪需要视口信息——由 Game 侧 update 先暂存；spawn 时跳过（池小、
    // 视口裁剪在 update/draw 双重兜底），此处仅密度池满兜底
    let d: VDust | null = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < POOL; i++) {
      const c = this.pool[(this.cursor + i) % POOL];
      if (!c.active) { d = c; this.cursor = (this.cursor + i + 1) % POOL; break; }
    }
    if (!d) return null;
    const w2 = Math.max(5, w), h2 = Math.max(5, h);
    d.active = true;
    d.type = type;
    d.noGravity = false;
    d.color = color;
    d.alpha = alpha;
    d.x = x + (Math.random() * (w2 - 4) | 0) + 4;
    d.y = y + (Math.random() * (h2 - 4) | 0) + 4;
    d.vx = ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.1 + speedX;
    d.vy = ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.1 + speedY;
    const f = frameOf(type);
    d.fx = f.fx; d.fy = f.fy;
    d.rotation = 0;
    d.scale = (1 + ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.01) * scale;
    d.noLight = false;
    d.fadeIn = 0;
    d.noLightEmittance = false;
    d.followPlayer = false;
    d.followOnce = false;
    d.dyeVid = -1;
    // :221-226 烟雾族初速特例（本批 type 6 命中；59-65 等后续接入同享）
    if (type === 228 || type === 279 || type === 269 || type === 135 || type === 6
      || type === 242 || type === 75 || type === 169 || type === 29 || (type >= 59 && type <= 65)
      || type === 158 || (type >= 293 && type <= 298) || type === 302 || type === 307 || type === 310) {
      d.vy = ((Math.random() * 16 | 0) - 10) * 0.1;
      d.vx *= 0.3;
      d.scale *= 0.7;
    }
    return d;
  }

        for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)

}

  // XNA Color×float 同乘 RGBA → 预乘混合下 rgb:alpha = 2:1（主弹幕批次
  // BlendState.AlphaBlend 预乘语义，Main.cs:22032）→ canvas 精确等价两 pass：
  //   source-over α=f/2（背景衰减）+ lighter α=f/2（加色）＝ trailColor×f 全亮
  const n = this.trail.length / 3;
  if (n >= 2) {
    const col = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;
    for (let i = n - 1; i >= 1; i--) {          // 新→旧（GPU 四边形序）
      const f = fractalStripFade((n - 1 - (i - 0.5)) / (60 - 1));
      if (f <= 0.02) continue;
      const x1 = this.trail[i * 3], y1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 1];
      const r1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 2];
      const x0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 3], y0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 2], r0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 1];
      // 截面端点（± 刃轴向 × trailWidth）
      const d1x = Math.cos(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d1y = Math.sin(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;
      const d0x = Math.cos(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d0y = Math.sin(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x1 + d1x, y1 + d1y);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 + d0x, y0 + d0y);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 - d0x, y0 - d0y);
      ctx.lineTo(x1 - d1x, y1 - d1y);
      ctx.closePath();
      const a = 0.5 * f;
      ctx.fillStyle = col;
      ctx.globalAlpha = a; ctx.fill();                       // 覆盖 pass
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
      ctx.globalAlpha = a; ctx.fill();                       // 加色 pass
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
  }
  ...
}
```

Wait — the progress index: entry i (our array) corresponds to vanilla index j = n−1−i. Quad between entries i (newer) and i−1 (older): vanilla quads connect pairs (j, j+1) — j from 0. Our quad (i−1, i) = vanilla (j_i = n−1−i, j_i+1 = n−1−(i−1) = j_i + 1). ✓. Progress at quad mid: (j_i + 0.5)/59 where j_i = n−1−i. So f = fade((n−1−i+0.5)/59) = fade((n−0.5−i)/59). My expression above `(n - 1 - (i - 0.5)) / 59` = (n−1−i+0.5)/59 ✓ same.

Note the full-length progress divisor: vanilla `num2 = expectedVertexPairsAmount ?? num` = oldPos.Length = 60 (array length, NOT TrailCacheLength... they're both 60). progress = i/(num2−1) = i/59. When the trail is only partially filled (n < 60), vanilla's array is ALWAYS length 60 with zero-filled tail — PrepareStrip stops at first zero → uses only n valid entries, but progress STILL divides by 59! So early in flight, the strip's progress spans 0..(n−1)/59 — the newest is 0 and oldest only reaches (n−1)/59 < 0.98 → fade barely starts! The young trail is nearly FULL COLOR (no fade) — ribbon bright from player to sword. As history fills, the tail reaches progress 0.98 and fades.

CRITICAL: my formula (n−0.5−i)/59 with CURRENT n handles this naturally (oldest entry progress = (n−1)/59). ✓ — as long as I divide by 59 always, not n−1.

Our current implementation divides by n (`fade = (i+1)/n`) — wrong for young trails (fades too early). Good catch — with the /59 denominator, a 5-entry trail has max progress 4/59=0.068 → f≈0.93 — nearly full color. ✓ faithful.

```ts
private drawIconAt(ctx, r, prof, x, y, rot, mirror, scale, alpha, additive = false) {
  const icon = ...vicon(this.ai1);
  ctx.save();
  if (additive) ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(rot);
  if (mirror) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  if (icon) ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, -icon.sw*scale/2, -icon.sh*scale/2, icon.sw*scale, icon.sh*scale);
  else { fallback rect }
  ctx.restore();
}
```

/** StripColors 衰减因子（FinalFractalHelper.cs:56-58 逐字）：
 *  f(progress) = 1 − GetLerpValue(0, 0.98, progress) */
export function fractalStripFade(progress: number): number {
  const v = (progress - 0) / (0.98 - 0);
  return 1 - Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
}
```

```ts
/** StripColors 衰减因子（FinalFractalHelper.cs:54-59 逐字）：
 *  result = trailColor × (1 − GetLerpValue(0, 0.98, progressOnStrip))，A 再减半——
 *  XNA Color×float 四通道同乘。返回该乘数 f（rgb 与 A 同乘，A 额外 ×1/2）。 */
export function fractalStripFade(progress: number): number {
  const v = progress / 0.98;
  return 1 - Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
}

/** 档案查询（GetFinalFractalProfile :192-199：缺项回退 50f/White） */
export function fractalProfile(id: number): { len: number; color: [number, number, number] } {
  return FRACTAL_PROFILES[id] ?? { len: 50, color: [255, 255, 255] };
}

/** 白残影 alpha（Main.cs:30606-30611）：White × GetLerpValue(0,60,60−num159)
 *  × Opacity × GetLerpValue(0,20,counter−num159) × GetLerpValue(68,60,counter−num159) */
export function fractalGhostAlpha(counter: number, num159: number, opacity: number): number {
  const t = counter - num159;
  return ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 60, 60 - num159) * opacity * ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 20, t) * ZenithSword.lerpV(68, 60, t);
}
```
lerpV is private static — fractalGhostAlpha references ZenithSword.lerpV — TS private static accessible within the same module? No — `private static` on the class is only accessible within the class body. Module-level function can't access it. Make lerpV non-private (keep static)? Changing `private static lerpV` to `static lerpV` — tests access z['counter'] via bracket anyway. Making it public static is a minor API surface addition — fine.

Actually the helper functions are defined BEFORE the class (FRACTAL_PROFILES is at 510, class at 539) — function hoisting: `function` declarations hoist, so referencing ZenithSword inside function bodies is fine at call time. But placing them before the class is fine since calls happen later.

Now the draw method — full rewrite:

```ts
draw(r: { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement; atlas?: unknown }): void {
  const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return;
  const prof = fractalProfile(this.ai1);
  const tw = prof.len / 2;                       // trailWidth = 全刃长/2（StripWidth :61-64 恒宽）
  const icon = (r.atlas as unknown as { vicon?: (id: number) => { img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } | null } | undefined)?.vicon?.(this.ai1) ?? null;
  const num182 = ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 20, this.counter) * ZenithSword.lerpV(68, 60, this.counter);

  // ① 彩带拖尾 ...
  const n = this.trail.length / 3;
  if (n >= 2) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;
    for (let i = n - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      const f = fractalStripFade((n - 0.5 - i) / (60 - 1));  // progress = i/(Length−1)（:63）
      if (f <= 0.02) continue;
      const x1 = this.trail[i * 3], y1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 1], r1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 2];
      const x0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 3], y0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 2], r0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 1];
      const d1x = Math.cos(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d1y = Math.sin(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;
      const d0x = Math.cos(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d0y = Math.sin(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x1 + d1x, y1 + d1y);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 + d0x, y0 + d0y);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 - d0x, y0 - d0y);
      ctx.lineTo(x1 - d1x, y1 - d1y);
      ctx.closePath();
      const a = 0.5 * f;                          // A = (255×f)/2（result.A /= 2）
      ctx.globalAlpha = a;
      ctx.fill();                                 // 覆盖 pass（预乘的 dst×(1−f/2)）
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
      ctx.fill();                                 // 加色 pass（rgb 合计 = trailColor×f）
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.restore();
  }
  // ②③④⑤ ...
  const drawIcon = (x: number, y: number, rot: number, mirror: boolean, scale: number, alpha: number, additive = false) => {
    if (alpha <= 0.01) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (additive) ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));
    ctx.translate(x, y);
    ctx.rotate(rot);
    if (mirror) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
    if (icon) {
      const dw = icon.sw * scale, dh = icon.sh * scale;
      ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, -dw / 2, -dh / 2, dw, dh);
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;
      ctx.fillRect(-16 * scale, -16 * scale, 32 * scale, 32 * scale);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  };
  // ② 白残影 ×3（num159=60 越界跳过）
  for (let num159 = 60; num159 > 0; num159 -= 15) {
    if (num159 >= 60) continue;
    const j = n - 1 - num159;
    if (j < 0) continue;
    drawIcon(this.trail[j * 3], this.trail[j * 3 + 1],
      this.trail[j * 3 + 2] - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir,
      (this.trailDirs[j] === -1) !== (this.spriteDir === -1), 1,
      fractalGhostAlpha(this.counter, num159, this.opacity));
  }
  // ③ 本体（White×Opacity×num182）
  drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1, 1, this.opacity * num182);
  // ④ 1.25× 白叠绘两 pass
  const ov = this.opacity * num182;
  drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1, 1.25, 127 / 255 * ov);
  drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1, 1.25, 128 / 255 * ov, true);
  // ⑤ 刃尖星芒
  if (this.opacity > 0.01 && num182 > 0.01) {
    prettyStarSparkle(ctx, this.opacity,
      this.cx + Math.cos(this.rot - Math.PI / 2) * tw,
      this.cy + Math.sin(this.rot - Math.PI / 2) * tw,
      [255, 255, 255], prof.color, this.counter, 15, 30, 30, 45, 0, [5, 2], [1, 1], num182, num182);
  }
}
```

Wait — issue with drawIcon closure referencing `icon` and `prof` — fine. But drawIcon was previously defined INSIDE draw with ctx captured — same.

One issue: old code did `ctx.globalAlpha = 1;` after drawIcon outside restore — I use save/restore properly now.

Hmm — num182 vs num186: both computed with identical formula/args (t2 and t3 both = localAI[0], same lerp bounds) — same value. Use one variable, note the identity.

Now — about `this.opacity * num182` for the main sprite: vanilla color44 = GetAlpha(projectileColor) × num182 where GetAlpha returns White × Opacity. XNA: White*Opacity multiplies rgb AND alpha by Opacity → then ×num182 again all channels: rgb factor = Opacity*num182, alpha = 255*Opacity*num182/255 = Opacity*num182. rgb:alpha = 1:1 → single source-over with globalAlpha = Opacity*num182. ✓ 

White overlay: (255,255,255,127)*Opacity*num186: rgb factor = Opacity*num186, alpha = 127*Opacity*num186/255. rgb:alpha = 255/127 = 2.008 → two-pass: source-over α = (127/255)*ov, lighter α = (1 − 127/255)*ov = (128/255)*ov. ✓

Afterimage: White*lv3*Opacity*num170 — 1:1 → single pass. ✓

Sparkle — prettyStarSparkle handles internally. ✓

const bdx = Math.cos(this.rot - Math.PI / 2), bdy = Math.sin(this.rot - Math.PI / 2);
const dist = (prof.len / 2) * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5);
const px = this.cx + bdx * dist, py = this.cy + bdy * dist;
const dAng = this.rot - Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI / 2 * this.spriteDir;
const wf = Math.random() * 0.3;
const col = { r: Math.round(prof.color[0] + (255 - prof.color[0]) * wf), g: Math.round(prof.color[1] + (255 - prof.color[1]) * wf), b: Math.round(prof.color[2] + (255 - prof.color[2]) * wf), a: 255 };
for (let k = 0; k < cnt; k++) {

 "new_string": "/** GetRandomProfileIndex（:170-180）：档案表随机，命中 4956 移除后重掷一次\n *  （重掷池不含 4956——曾从全表重掷仍有 1/21 概率回到 Zenith） */\nexport function randomFractalProfile(excludeZenith = true): number {\n  const keys = Object.keys(FRACTAL_PROFILES).map(Number);\n  let id = keys[Math.floor(Math.random() * keys.length)];\n  if (excludeZenith && id === 4956) {\n    const rest = keys.filter((k) => k !== 4956);\n    id = rest[Math.floor(Math.random() * rest.length)];\n  }\n  return id;\n}\n\n/** GetFinalFractalProfile（:192-199）：缺项回退 50f/Color.White */\nexport function fractalProfile(id: number): { len: number; color: [number, number, number] } {\n  return FRACTAL_PROFILES[id] ?? { len: 50, color: [255, 255, 255] };\n}\n\n/** 拖尾衰减因子（FinalFractalProfile.StripColors :54-59 逐字）：\n *  result = trailColor × (1 − GetLerpValue(0, 0.98, progressOnStrip))；result.A /= 2。\n *  XNA Color×float 四通道同乘 → rgb 与 A 同乘本因子、A 额外减半（预乘混合下\n *  rgb:alpha = 2:1——draw 的两 pass 精确等价来源）。progress = i/(oldPos.Length−1)\n *  = i/59（VertexStrip.PrepareStrip :63，恒除 60−1——幼年拖尾不缩分母）。 */\nexport function fractalStripFade(progress: number): number {\n  return 1 - Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress / 0.98));\n}\n\n/** 白残影 alpha（Main.cs:30606-30611）：Color.White × GetLerpValue(0, 60, 60−num159)\n *  × Opacity × GetLerpValue(0,20,counter−num159) × GetLerpValue(68,60,counter−num159)\n *  （rgb:alpha = 1:1 → 单 pass source-over；t = 历史时刻的 localAI[0] −− 原版按\n *  索引差近似，非真实计数差——逐字沿用） */\nexport function fractalGhostAlpha(counter: number, num159: number, opacity: number): number {\n  const t = counter - num159;\n  return ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 60, 60 - num159) * opacity\n    * ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 20, t) * ZenithSword.lerpV(68, 60, t);\n}"
}

 "new_string": "  private step(game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (this.counter === 0) game.playSfxFiles(['Item_169'], 1, this.cx, this.cy);   // :40439\n    // TrailingMode 4 记录（:15841-15843）：oldPos[0]/oldRot[0]/oldSpriteDirection[0]\n    // 在 Projectile.Update 内先于 AI_182 压栈——记录上一 update 末态\n    this.trail.push(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot);\n    if (this.trail.length > 60 * 3) this.trail.splice(0, this.trail.length - 60 * 3);\n    this.trailDirs.push(this.spriteDir);\n    if (this.trailDirs.length > 60) this.trailDirs.splice(0, this.trailDirs.length - 60);\n    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);\n    const lv = ZenithSword.lerpV(900, 0, spd * 2);      // GetLerpValue(900,0,|vel|×2)\n    this.counter += 0.7 + (2 - 0.7) * lv;               // Lerp(0.7,2,lv)\n    if (this.counter >= 120) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    const phase = ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 1, this.counter / 60);\n    const va = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);\n    const num6 = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n    const num7 = Math.PI + num6 * phase * Math.PI * 2;\n    let num8 = spd + ZenithSword.lerpV(0.5, 1, phase) * 40;\n    if (num8 < 60) num8 = 60;\n    // 锚点 = MountedCenter + vel（随玩家移动——:40466）\n    const ax = this.owner.cx + this.vx, ay = this.owner.cy + this.vy;\n    // 椭圆轨道（:40474-40478）+ 起始回拉（vector3 :40479-40480）\n    const spinX = Math.cos(num7) * num8;\n    const spinY = Math.sin(num7) * this.ai0 * (2 + (1 - 2) * lv);\n    const ca = Math.cos(va), sa = Math.sin(va);\n    const pull = (1 - ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 0.5, phase));\n    const pullX = pull * num6 * -num8 * 0.1;\n    const pullY = pull * -this.ai0 * 0.3;\n    this.x = ax + (ca * spinX - sa * spinY) + pullX - this.w / 2;\n    this.y = ay + (sa * spinX + ca * spinY) + pullY - this.h / 2;\n    this.rot = num7 + va + Math.PI / 2;\n    this.spriteDir = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n    if (this.ai0 < 0) {\n      this.rot = Math.PI + num6 * phase * (-Math.PI * 2) + va + Math.PI / 2;   // 反旋（:40488-40494）\n      this.spriteDir = this.vx > 0 ? -1 : 1;\n    }\n    this.opacity = ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 5, this.counter) * ZenithSword.lerpV(120, 115, this.counter);\n    const prof = fractalProfile(this.ai1);\n    // 光照（:40493-40496，num2<1 门 :40478）：Center 点 trailColor×0.5×Opacity +\n    // MountedCenter 点 Lerp(Vector3.One, trailColor, 0.7)×Opacity×0.15（后者每剑\n    // 叠加——齐射时玩家侧聚成暖白光晕）\n    if (phase < 1) {\n      this.lightRGB = [prof.color[0] / 255 * 0.5 * this.opacity, prof.color[1] / 255 * 0.5 * this.opacity, prof.color[2] / 255 * 0.5 * this.opacity];\n      game.addLightAt?.(this.owner.cx, this.owner.cy,\n        (1 + (prof.color[0] / 255 - 1) * 0.7) * this.opacity * 0.15,\n        (1 + (prof.color[1] / 255 - 1) * 0.7) * this.opacity * 0.15,\n        (1 + (prof.color[2] / 255 - 1) * 0.7) * this.opacity * 0.15);\n    } else {\n      this.lightRGB = null;\n    }\n    // 拖尾尘（:40480-40492 调用点 → StripDust :38-52）：每 update num11 次独立掷\n    // Next(9)==0，命中出 Next(1,4)=1-3 粒尘 278（Dust.NewDustPerfect：alpha 100、\n    // color=Lerp(trailColor,White,rand×0.3)、scale 0.4、fadeIn 0.4+rand×0.3、\n    // noGravity、velocity += (rot−π/2+π/2·spriteDir)向×(3+rand×4)）；\n    // 位置 = Center + 刃向×trailWidth×Lerp(0.5,1,rand)（刃中段→刃尖）\n    if (phase < 1) {\n      let num11 = 1 + Math.floor(spd / 100);\n      num11 = Math.floor(num11 * ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 0.5, phase) * ZenithSword.lerpV(1, 0.5, phase));\n      if (num11 < 1) num11 = 1;\n      const bdx = Math.cos(this.rot - Math.PI / 2), bdy = Math.sin(this.rot - Math.PI / 2);\n      const dist = (prof.len / 2) * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5);\n      const px = this.cx + bdx * dist, py = this.cy + bdy * dist;\n      for (let i = 0; i < num11; i++) {\n        if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 9) !== 0) continue;      // Next(9)==0（1/9）\n        const dAng = this.rot - Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI / 2 * this.spriteDir;\n        for (let k = 0, cnt = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3); k < cnt; k++) {\n          const wf = Math.random() * 0.3;   // Color.Lerp(trailColor, White, rand×0.3)\n          // spawn() 对 w=0 的盒内散布退化为确定 +4（(rand×1|0)+4）→ 传 −4 抵消\n          const d = game.spawnDust?.(px - 4, py - 4, 0, 0, 278, 0, 0, 100,\n            {\n              r: Math.round(prof.color[0] + (255 - prof.color[0]) * wf),\n              g: Math.round(prof.color[1] + (255 - prof.color[1]) * wf),\n              b: Math.round(prof.color[2] + (255 - prof.color[2]) * wf),\n              a: 255,\n            }, 0.4, true);\n          if (!d) break;\n          d.scale = 0.4;\n          d.fadeIn = 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.3;\n          const dsp = 3 + Math.random() * 4;   // velocity += dir×(3+rand×4)（覆写池抖动）\n          d.vx = Math.cos(dAng) * dsp;\n          d.vy = Math.sin(dAng) * dsp;\n        }\n      }\n    }"
}

i < n - 1; i++) {\n        const fade = (i + 1) / n;          // 尾（最旧 i=0）→ 0、头（最新）→ 1\n        const a = 0.5 * fade * this.opacity;\n        if (a <= 0.02) continue;\n        ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]},${a})`;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(this.trail[i * 3], this.trail[i * 3 + 1]);\n        ctx.lineTo(this.trail[i * 3 + 3], this.trail[i * 3 + 4]);\n        ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      ctx.restore();\n    }\n    // 本体 = ai[1] 物品贴图（Main.cs:29945-29955：LoadItem(ai[1])、origin=中心、\n    // num152=−π/4×spriteDir 加进 rotation23；spriteDir=−1 配 FlipHorizontally——\n    // 局部系先镜像再旋转，刀尖恒指 (rot−π/2) 向（拖尾尘/辉光同向）。曾把\n    // num152 符号写反且不镜像：贴图垂直于轨迹 90°（2026-08-19 天顶剑审查）\n    const icon = (r.atlas as unknown as { vicon?: (id: number) => { img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } | null } | undefined)?.vicon?.(this.ai1);\n    const drawIcon = (scale: number, alpha: number) => {\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));\n      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n      ctx.rotate(this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir);\n      if (this.spriteDir === -1) ctx.scale(-1, 1);\n      if (icon) {\n        const dw = icon.sw * scale, dh = icon.sh * scale;\n        ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, -dw / 2, -dh / 2, dw, dh);\n      } else {\n        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;\n        ctx.fillRect(-16 * scale, -16 * scale, 32 * scale, 32 * scale);\n      }\n      ctx.restore();\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n    };\n    drawIcon(1, this.opacity);\n    // 1.25× 白色残影叠绘（Main.cs:30840-30842：(255,255,255,127)×Opacity×num186，\n    // num186=GetLerpValue(0,20,counter)×GetLerpValue(68,60,counter)）\n    const num186 = ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 20, this.counter) * ZenithSword.lerpV(68, 60, this.counter);\n    if (num186 > 0.02) drawIcon(1.25, this.opacity * num186 * 0.5);\n  }\n}",

  // trailWidth = 全刃长/2（StripWidth :61-64 恒宽——沿程无宽度变化）\n    const icon = (r.atlas as unknown as { vicon?: (id: number) => { img: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } | null } | undefined)?.vicon?.(this.ai1) ?? null;\n    // 本体淡入淡出包络（:30799-30801 num182 与 :30840 num186 同式同参 t=localAI[0]）\n    const num182 = ZenithSword.lerpV(0, 20, this.counter) * ZenithSword.lerpV(68, 60, this.counter);\n    const n = this.trail.length / 3;\n\n    // ---- ① 彩带拖尾（FinalFractalHelper.Draw :175-190 → VertexStrip :50-67）----\n    // 顶点对 = oldPos[i]+Size/2 ± (oldRot[i]−π/2)单位向 × trailWidth（AddVertexPair\n    // :153-159——截面沿【刃轴向】随剑旋转，非运动法向）；progressOnStrip =\n    // i/(60−1)（:63，恒除 59——幼年拖尾全程近满色）。颜色（StripColors :54-59\n    // 逐字）：result = trailColor × (1−GetLerpValue(0,0.98,progress))、result.A/=2\n    // ——XNA Color×float 四通道同乘 → 预乘混合（主弹幕批次 BlendState.AlphaBlend\n    // 预乘语义，Main.cs:22032；本项目 Renderer.ts:245 既定结论）下 rgb:alpha=2:1\n    // → canvas 两 pass 精确等价：source-over α=f/2（背景只衰减 f/2）+ lighter\n    // α=f/2（加色）——rgb 合计 = trailColor×f 全亮、无 Opacity 因子（剑亡即整条\n    // 消失，原版拖尾不随 Opacity 淡出）。四边形按 GPU 索引序新→旧叠画。\n    if (n >= 2) {\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;\n      for (let i = n - 1; i >= 1; i--) {\n        const f = fractalStripFade((n - 0.5 - i) / (60 - 1));\n        if (f <= 0.02) continue;\n        const x1 = this.trail[i * 3], y1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 1], r1 = this.trail[i * 3 + 2];\n        const x0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 3], y0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 2], r0 = this.trail[i * 3 - 1];\n        const d1x = Math.cos(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d1y = Math.sin(r1 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;\n        const d0x = Math.cos(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw, d0y = Math.sin(r0 - Math.PI / 2) * tw;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(x1 + d1x, y1 + d1y);\n        ctx.lineTo(x0 + d0x, y0 + d0y);\n        ctx.lineTo(x0 - d0x, y0 - d0y);\n        ctx.lineTo(x1 - d1x, y1 - d1y);\n        ctx.closePath();\n        const a = 0.5 * f;   // A = (255×f)/2（result.A /= 2）\n        ctx.globalAlpha = a;\n        ctx.fill();                                    // 覆盖 pass：dst×(1−f/2)\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n        ctx.fill();                                    // 加色 pass：+trailColor×f/2\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n      }\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n      ctx.restore();\n    }\n\n    // ---- 贴图绘制原语（:29945-29953：LoadItem(ai[1])、Frame() 全幅、origin=中心、\n    // 基角 −π/4×【当前】spriteDir 加进旋转；镜像=局部系 scale(−1,1)（XNA Flip源\n    // 矩形后旋转 = canvas 先 rotate 后镜像），刀尖恒指 (rot−π/2) 向）----\n    const drawIcon = (x: number, y: number, rot: number, mirror: boolean, scale: number, alpha: number, additive = false) => {\n      if (alpha <= 0.01) return;\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n      if (additive) ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));\n      ctx.translate(x, y);\n      ctx.rotate(rot);\n      if (mirror) ctx.scale(-1, 1);\n      if (icon) {\n        const dw = icon.sw * scale, dh = icon.sh * scale;\n        ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx, icon.sy, icon.sw, icon.sh, -dw / 2, -dh / 2, dw, dh);\n      } else {\n        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${prof.color[0]},${prof.color[1]},${prof.color[2]})`;\n        ctx.fillRect(-16 * scale, -16 * scale, 32 * scale, 32 * scale);\n      }\n      ctx.restore();\n    };\n\n    // ---- ② 白残影 ×3（:30381 循环 + :30174-30181 933 档：num156=60、num154=0、\n    // num155=−15 → num159∈{60,45,30,15}；60 ≥ oldPos.Length=60 越界跳过 :30383）。\n    // 色（:30606-30611，替换通用乘子）：White × GetLerpValue(0,60,60−num159) ×\n    // Opacity × num170(counter−num159)（rgb:alpha=1:1 单 pass）；旋转 = oldRot +\n    // (−π/4×当前 spriteDir)（:30614 num152 取现值）；镜像 = 历史与当前\n    // spriteDirection 异号（:30572-30577 XOR）；scale = Lerp(1,1,·)=1 ----\n    for (let num159 = 60; num159 > 0; num159 -= 15) {\n      if (num159 >= 60) continue;\n      const j = n - 1 - num159;\n      if (j < 0) continue;\n      drawIcon(this.trail[j * 3], this.trail[j * 3 + 1],\n        this.trail[j * 3 + 2] - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir,\n        (this.trailDirs[j] === -1) !== (this.spriteDir === -1), 1,\n        fractalGhostAlpha(this.counter, num159, this.opacity));\n    }\n    // ---- ③ 本体（:30761-30803）：GetAlpha(:76047-76050)=White×Opacity ×num182\n    // （rgb:alpha=1:1 → 单 pass source-over，不受场景光照染色的全亮白）----\n    drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1,\n      1, this.opacity * num182);\n    // ---- ④ 1.25× 白叠绘（:30839-30841）：色 (255,255,255,127)×Opacity×num186——\n    // 预乘 rgb:alpha = 255:127 → 两 pass：source-over α=(127/255)·ov（覆盖）\n    // + lighter α=(128/255)·ov（加色）＝ rgb×ov 全量、背景衰减仅 (127/255)·ov ----\n    const ov = this.opacity * num182;\n    drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1,\n      1.25, 127 / 255 * ov);\n    drawIcon(this.cx, this.cy, this.rot - Math.PI / 4 * this.spriteDir, this.spriteDir === -1,\n      1.25, 128 / 255 * ov, true);\n    // ---- ⑤ 刃尖星芒（:30842-30845）：DrawPrettyStarSparkle(Opacity, dir, Center+\n    // (rot−π/2)向×trailWidth, White×num186, trailColor(A/2)×num186, localAI[0],\n    // 15,30,30,45, 0, (5,2), (1,1))——15-45 counter 窗内十字星芒 ----\n    if (this.opacity > 0.01 && num182 > 0.01) {\n      prettyStarSparkle(ctx, this.opacity,\n        this.cx + Math.cos(this.rot - Math.PI / 2) * tw,\n        this.cy + Math.sin(this.rot - Math.PI / 2) * tw,\n        [255, 255, 255], prof.color, this.counter,\n        15, 30, 30, 45, 0, [5, 2], [1, 1], num182, num182);\n    }\n  }\n}"

336	    const owner = { cx: 50 * TILE + 10, cy: 90 * TILE + 20, facing: 1, dead: false };
337	    const saw = new ChainsawProj(owner as never, 120, 5.5,
338	      () => ch, () => ({ x: 55 * TILE, y: 90 * TILE }));
339	    saw.critChance = 0;                                   // 禁暴击（120 基伤断言确定性）
340	    saw.fixedUpdate(1, game);
341	    expect(saw.x + saw.w / 2).toBeCloseTo(owner.cx, 0);   // 挂手锚定
342	    const f0 = saw['frame'];
343	    saw.fixedUpdate(1, game); saw.fixedUpdate(1, game);
344	    expect(saw['frame']).toBe(1 - f0);                     // 2t/帧交替
345	    expect(dmgTaken).toBeGreaterThan(0);                   // 持续命中（40×40 覆盖弹盒）
346	    const d0 = dmgTaken;
347	    for (let t = 0; t < 5; t++) saw.fixedUpdate(1, game);
348	    expect(dmgTaken).toBe(d0);                             // 10t 冷却内不重复（首击后 5t 仍在窗内）
349	    ch = false;
350	    saw.fixedUpdate(1, game);
351	    expect(saw.dead).toBe(true);                           // 断链即亡
352	  });
353	});
354

751	    if (x2 >= minX && x2 <= maxX && y2 >= minY && y2 <= maxY) return true;

756	      const r = q / p;
757	      if (p < 0) { if (r > t1) return false; if (r > t0) t0 = r; }

import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
const browser = await puppeteer.launch({executablePath:'/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless:'new', args:['--no-sandbox'], defaultViewport:{width:1280,height:800}});
const page = await browser.newPage();
const errs = [];
page.on('pageerror', e => errs.push(e.message));
await page.goto('http://localhost:5199', {waitUntil:'networkidle0'});
await page.type('input', 'swingtest');
await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('select').selectedIndex = 0);
await page.click('button');
await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__swGame && window.__swGame.world, {timeout:120000});
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500));
// 选中铁剑（槽 2），按住左键 3 秒，数挥击次数
const out = await page.evaluate(async () => {
  const g = window.__swGame;
  g.player.inv.selected = 2; // 铁剑
  g.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  // 模拟按住鼠标（直接调 updateUse 不便 → 用 canvas mousedown 事件）
  const cv = document.querySelector('canvas');
  const rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  const opts = { clientX: rect.left + rect.width / 2 + 200, clientY: rect.top + rect.height / 2, bubbles: true };
  cv.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown', opts));
  let swings = 0, lastT = null;
  const t0 = performance.now();
  await new Promise(res => {
    const check = () => {
      const s = g.swing;
      if (s && s !== lastT) { lastT = s; swings++; }
      if (performance.now() - t0 < 3000) requestAnimationFrame(check); else res();
    };
    requestAnimationFrame(check);
  });
  cv.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup', opts));
  return { swingsIn3s: swings, perSec: +(swings / 3).toFixed(2) };
});
console.log(JSON.stringify({ out, errs: errs.slice(0,5) }, null, 1));
await browser.close();
